ca kaḥ śaktaḥ tāni nirṇetum vāgīśaḥ vā maheśvaraḥ deva kāryam kṛtam tena agnayaḥ vidhivat hutāḥ vedāḥ adhītāḥ catvāraḥ yena revā avagāhitā prādhānyāt ca api saṃkṣepāt tīrthāni uktāni te mayā na śakyaḥ vistaraḥ pārtha śrotum vaktum ca vai mayā yudhiṣṭhiraḥ uvāca vidhānam ca yamān ca eva niyamān ca vadasva me śrī mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca māsa ṛtu darvī parighaṭṭanena bhūtāni kālaḥ pacati iti vārtā santi iha vividha upāyāḥ nṛṇām deha viśodhanāḥ tīrtha sevā samam nā asti sva śarīrasya śodhanam kṛcchra cāndrāyaṇāt yaiḥ vā dvitīyam tīrtha sevayā yadā tīrtham samuddiśya prayāti puruṣaḥ nṛpa paramāḥ moda pūrṇāḥ te prayānti asya anuyāyinaḥ iṣṭa bandhūn ca viṣṇum ca śaṅkaram sa gaṇeśvaram vrajet dvija abhyanujñātaḥ gṛhītvā niyamān api eka aśanam brahmacaryam bhū śayyām satya vādi tām snānam sura arcanam ca eva śrāddhe vai piṇḍapātanam viprāṇām bhojanam śaktyā sarva tīrtheṣu ayam vidhiḥ eka aśanam brahmacaryam a kṣāra lavaṇa aśanam snātvā tīrtha abhigamanam haviṣya eka anna bhojanam varjayet patita ālāpam bahu bhāṣaṇam eva ca parīvādam para annam ca nīca saṅgam vivarjayet vrajet ca nirupānatkaḥ vasānaḥ vāsasī śuciḥ tīrthe gatvā tathā snātvā kṛtvā ca eva sura arcanam arthavāda bhavām śaṅkām vihāya bharata ṛṣabha kṛtvā vicāram śāstra uktam parikalpya yathocitam kāyena kṛcchra caraṇe hi aśaktānām viśuddhaye jñātvā tīrtha aviśeṣam hi prāyaścittam samācaret tat śṛṇuṣva mahā rāja narmadāyām yathocitam caturviṃśati kṛcchrāṇām phalam āpnoti śobhanam atas ūrdhvam yojaneṣu pādakṛcchram udāhṛtaḥ praṇava ākhye mahā rāja tathā revā urisaṃgame bhṛgukṣetre tathā gatvā phalam tat dviguṇam smṛtam saṅgame devanadyāḥ ca śūlabhede nṛpa uttama dviguṇam pāda hīnam syāt tathā oṃkāre ca mahā rāja tat api syāt samañjasam saṅgameṣu tathā anyāsām nadīnām revayā saha prāhuḥ te sārdha kṛcchram vai phalam pūrvam yudhiṣṭhira triguṇam kṛcchram āpnoti revā sāgara saṅgame revā sevā samācāraḥ saṃyuktaḥ śuddha buddhimān iti te kathitam pārtha prāyaścitta artha lakṣaṇam yudhiṣṭhiraḥ uvāca yat jñātvā niścitam me syāt manaḥ śuddheḥ tu kāraṇam mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca śṛṇu pāṇḍava vakṣyāmi pramāṇam yojanasya yat tathā yātrā viśeṣeṇa viśeṣam kṛcchra sambhavam tiryagyavodarāṇi aṣṭau ūrdhvāḥ vā vrīhayaḥ trayaḥ pramāṇam aṅgulasya āhuḥ vitastiḥ dvādaśa aṃgulā saḥ eva daṇḍaḥ gaditaḥ viśeṣa jñaiḥ yudhiṣṭhira tat me śṛṇu mahīpāla śraddadhānāya kathyate yasmin tīrthe hi yat proktam phalam kṛcchra ādikam nṛpa tatra api upoṣaṇāt kṛcchra phalam prāpnoti atha adhikam tatra vikhyāta deveśam snātvā dṛṣṭvā abhipūjya ca praṇamya labhate pārtha phalam kṛcchra bhavam sudhīḥ tīrthe mukhya phalam snānāt dvitīyam ca api upoṣaṇāt tṛtīyam khyāta devasya darśana abhyarcana ādibhiḥ pañcamam sarva tīrtheṣu kalpanīyam hi dūratas tīra sthaḥ yojanāt arvāk daśa aṃśam labhate phalam api arvāk yojanāt pārtha dadyāt kṛcchra phalam nṛṇām ṣaṣ yojana vahā nadyaḥ alpāḥ dvādaśa eva ca iti śrī skānde mahāpurāṇe ekāśīti sāhasryām saṃhitāyām pañcame āvantya khaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe tīrthayātrādividhānaviśeṣakathanam nāma saptaviṃśati adhika dviśatatamaḥ adhyāyaḥ yudhiṣṭhiraḥ uvāca para artham tīrthayātrāyām gacchataḥ kasya kim phalam kiyat mātram muni śreṣṭha tat me brūhi kṛpā nidhe mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca para artham gacchataḥ tat me vadataḥ śṛṇu pārthiva śarīrasya athavā śaktyā anyat vā kārya yogataḥ śreṣṭham hi vihitam prāhuḥ dharma karma yudhiṣṭhira na śūdrāya matim dadyāt na ucchiṣṭam na haviṣkṛtam devatā ārādhanam dīkṣā strī śūdra patanāni ṣaḍ patyau sarvam kuryāt anujñayā gatvā para artham tīrtha ādau ṣoḍaśa aṃśa phalam labhet anusaṅgena tīrthasya snāne snāna phalam viduḥ na eva yātrā phalam tad jñāḥ śāstra uktam kalmaṣa apaham poṣaka arthāt ayoḥ ca api mātāmahyāḥ guroḥ tathā svasuḥ mātṛṣvasuḥ paitryāḥ ācārya adhyāpakasya ca ityādi arthe naraḥ snātvā svayam aṣṭa aṃśam āpnuyāt sākṣāt pitroḥ prakurvāṇaḥ caturtha aṃśam avāpnuyāt pati patnyoḥ mithas ca arddham phalam prāhuḥ manīṣiṇaḥ ṣaṣ tri pañca catur bhāgān phalam āpnoti vai naraḥ varṣā ṛtu samāyoge sarvāḥ nadyaḥ rajasvalāḥ muktvā sarasvatīm gaṅgām narmadām yamunā nadīm iti śrī skānde mahāpurāṇe ekāśīti sāhasryām saṃhitāyām pañcame āvantya khaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe parārthatīrthayātrāphalakathanam nāma aṣṭaviṃśati adhika dviśatatamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī mārkaṇḍeyaḥ uvāca śiva prītyā yathā proktam vāyunā deva saṃsadi mayā dvādaśa sāhasrī saṃhitā yā śrutā purā pṛṣṭaḥ tvayā aham bhūpāla parvate amarakaṇṭake sthitaḥ saṃkṣepataḥ sarvam mayā tat kathitam tava narmadā caritam puṇyam śṛṇu tasya asti yat phalam carite tu śrute devyāḥ labhate tādṛśam phalam sakṛt snātvā tathā śrutvā narmadāyām phalam hi tat ādi madhya avasānena narmadā caritam śubham sa prāpya śiva saṃsthānam rudra kanyā samāvṛtaḥ gṛhe vā tiṣṭhate yasya cāturvarṇyasya bhārata devatānām gurum śāstram paramam siddhi kāraṇam dakṣiṇe ca uttare kūle yāni tīrthāni kānicid sparśanāt darśanāt teṣām kīrtanāt śravaṇāt tathā saubhāgyam santatim svargam nārī śrutvā āpnuyāt dhanam māhātmyam narmadāyāḥ tu śrutvā pāpa bahiṣkṛtāḥ ye punar bhāvitātmānaḥ śṛṇvanti satatam nṛpa pūjayante idam devāḥ pūjitāḥ guravaḥ ca taiḥ narmadā pūjitā tena bhagavān ca maheśvaraḥ lekhāpayitvā sakalam narmadā caritam śubham etat purāṇam rudra uktam mahā puṇya phala pradam paṭhatām śṛṇvatām rājan sarva kāma artha siddhi dam go brāhmaṇebhyaḥ svasti astu dharmam dharmātmaja āśrayaḥ naraka anta karī revā sa tīrthā viśva pāvanī narmadā dharma dā ca astu śarma dā pārtha te sadā iti śrī skānde mahāpurāṇe ekāśīti sāhasryām saṃhitāyām pañcame āvantya khaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe mārkaṇḍeya yudhiṣṭhira saṃvāde revākhaṇḍapaṭhanaśravaṇadānādiphalaśrutivarṇanam nāma ekonatriṃśat adhika dviśatatamaḥ adhyāyaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca iti uktvā upararāma atha pāṇḍoḥ putrāya vai muniḥ mṛkaṇḍa tanayaḥ dhīmān sapta kalpa smaraḥ puras mārkaṇḍa muninā proktam yathā pārthāya sattamāḥ iyam puṇyā sarit śreṣṭhā revā viśva eka pāvanī oṅkāra jaladhim yāvat uvāca bhṛgunandanaḥ tathā api atra muni śreṣṭhāḥ proktam pārthāya vai yathā tīrtham oṃkāram ārabhya vakṣye tīrtha āvalim śubhām procyamānām samāsena tām śṛṇudhvam mahā ṛṣayaḥ natvā somam maheśānam natvā brahma acyutau ubhau sarasvatīm gaṇa īśānam devyāsa aṅghri pañkajam pūrva ācāryān tathā sarvān dṛṣṭvā adṛṣṭa artha vedinaḥ praṇamya narmadām devīm vakṣye tīrtha āvalim tu imām om namaḥ viśvarūpāya oṅkārāya akhilātmane yam ārabhye pravakṣyāmi revā tīrtha āvalim dvijāḥ asmin mārkaṇḍa gadite revā tīrtha krame śubhe purāṇa saṃhitā adhyāyāḥ mārkaṇḍa āśrama varṇanam tatas praśna adhikāraḥ ca praśaṃsā narmadā udbhavā tathā pañcadaśānām ca pravāhānām prakīrtanam mārkaṇḍeya anubhūtānām saptānām lakṣaṇāni ca māhātmyam ca eva revāyāḥ śiva viṣṇvoḥ tathā eva ca saṃhāra lakṣaṇam tadvat oṅkārasya ca sambhavaḥ tathā eva oṃkāra māhātmyam amarakaṇṭa kīrtanam amareśvaratīrtham ca tathā dāru vanam mahat dārukeśvaratīrtham ca tīrtham vai carukeśvaram carukā saṅgamaḥ tathā pātāleśvaratīrtham ca koṭiyajña āhvayam tathā varuṇeśvaratīrtham ca liṅgāni aṣṭa uttaram śatam sārasvatam ca aṣṭa rudram sāvitram soma saṃjñitam śivakhātam mahā tīrtham rudrāvartam dvijottamāḥ pippalāvarta tīrtham ca pippalyāḥ ca eva saṅgamaḥ viśalyā sambhavaḥ ca api bhṛgutuṅga adri kīrtanam viśalyā saṅgamaḥ puṇyaḥ karamardā samāgamaḥ saṅgamaḥ nīlagaṅgāyāḥ vidhvaṃsaḥ tripurasya ca kīrtanam tīrtha dānānām madhuka tṛtīyā vratam tīrtham jvāleśvaram nāma jvālāyāḥ saṅgamaḥ tathā ambarīṣasya tīrtham ca mahākāleśvaram tathā mātṛkeśvaratīrtham ca bhṛgutuṅga anuvarṇanam caṇḍapāṇeḥ ca māhātmyam kāverī saṅgamaḥ tathā kubereśvaratīrtham ca vārāhīsaṅgamaḥ tathā saṅgamaḥ caṇḍavegāyāḥ tīrtham caṇḍeśvaram tathā eraṇḍīsaṅgamaḥ puṇyaḥ eraṇḍeśvaram uttamam māhātmyam pañca liṅgānām oṅkārasya muni īśvarāḥ koṭitīrthasya māhātmyam tīrtham kākahradam tathā tīrtha lakṣam viduḥ pūrve kapilāyāḥ tu saṅgame vyavasthānam śarīrasya go pradāna anuvarṇanam aśokeśvaratīrtham ca mataṅgeśvaram uttamam tathā mṛgavanam puṇyam tatra tīrtham manoratham saṅgamaḥ aṅgāragartāyāḥ aṅgāreśvaram uttamam tathā meghavanam tīrtham devyāḥ nāma anukīrtanam saṅgamaḥ ca api kubjāyāḥ tīrtham kubjeśvaram tathā bilvāmrakam tathā tīrtham pūrṇadvīpam atas param tathā hiraṇyagarbhāyāḥ saṅgamaḥ puṇya kīrtanaḥ dvīpeśvaram nāma tīrtham puṇyam yajñeśvaram tathā māṇḍavya āśrama tīrtham ca viśokā saṅgamaḥ tathā vāgīśvaram nāma tīrtham puṇyaḥ vai vāgu saṅgamaḥ saṅgamaḥ ca sarasvatyāḥ īśānam tīrtham uttamam brahma udam śāṅkaram saumyam sārasvatam atas param sahasra yajña tīrtham ca kapālamocanam tathā tathā devapatham tīrtham tīrtham yajña sahasrakam śuklatīrtham dīptikeśam viṣṇutīrtham ca yodhanam narmadeśvara tīrtham ca varuṇeśam ca mārutam yogeśam rohiṇītīrtham dārutīrtham ca sattamāḥ brahmāvartam ca pattreśam vāhnam sauram ca kīrtyate meghanādam dārutīrtham devatīrtham guhāśrayam karañjeśam kuṇḍaleśam pippalādam atas param vimaleśvara tīrtham ca puṣkariṇyāḥ ca saṅgamaḥ praśaṃsā śūlabhedasya tatra eva andhaka vikramaḥ deva āśvāsana dānam ca tathā evā andhaka nigrahaḥ śūlabhedasya ca utpattiḥ tathā pātra parīkṣaṇam praśaṃsā dāna dharmasya ṛṣi śṛṅga anubhāvanam śabara svarga gamanam māhātmyam śūlabheda jam saṅgamaḥ mokṣanadyāḥ ca tīrtham ca vimaleśvaram tathā eva ulūkatīrtham ca puṣkariṇyāḥ ca saṅgamaḥ ādityeśvaratīrtham ca tīrtham vai saṅgameśvaram saṅgamaḥ bhīmakulyāyāḥ tīrtham bhīmeśvaram śubham mārkaṇḍeśvaratīrtham ca tathā vai pippaleśvaram karoṭīśvaratīrtham ca tīrtham indreśvaram śubham agastya īśam kumāra īśam vyāseśvaram anuttamam mātṛtīrtham ca muṇḍeśam cauram kāmeśvaram tathā saṅgamaḥ ca anuduhyāḥ vai tīrthe bhīmārjuna āhvaye tatas dhanadatīrtham ca jaṭeśam maṅgaleśvaram maṇināgeśvaram nāma maṇinadyāḥ ca saṅgamaḥ tatra eva mātṛtīrtham ca muninā uktam muni īśvarāḥ śaṅkhacūḍam ca kedāram pārāśaram atas param bhīmeśvaram ca candreśam aśvavatyāḥ ca saṅgamaḥ bahvīśvaram nāradeśam vaidyanātham kapīśvaram kumbheśvaram ca mārkaṇḍam rāmeśam lakṣmaṇeśvaram yamahāsāhvayam tīrtham tathā gaṅgeśvaram śubham nandikeśvara saṃjñam ca nara nārāyaṇa āhvayam naleśvaram ca mārkaṇḍam śuklatīrtham atas param vyāseśvaram param tīrtham tatra siddheśvaram tathā koṭitīrtham prabhātīrtham vāsukīśvaram uttamam saṅgamaḥ ca karañjāyāḥ mārkaṇḍeśvaram uttamam tīrtham koṭīśvaram nāma tathā saṃkarṣaṇa āhvayam kanakeśam manmatheśam tīrtham ca eva anasūyakam eraṇḍīsaṅgamaḥ puṇyaḥ mātṛtīrtham ca śobhanam tīrtham svarṇaśalākā ākhyam tathā ca eva ambikeśvaram karañjeśam bhārateśam nāgeśam mukuṭeśvaram rohiṇyam cakratīrtham ca uttareśvara saṃjñitam bhogeśvaram ca kedāram niṣkalaṅkam atas param mārkaṇḍam dhautapāpam ca tīrtham āṅgiraseśvaram koṭavīsaṅgamaḥ puṇyam koṭitīrtham ca tatra vai skāndam ca nārmadam brāhmam vālmīkeśvara saṃjñitam koṭitīrtham kapāleśam pāṇḍutīrtham trilocanam kapileśam kambukeśam prabhāsam kohaneśvaram indra īśam vālukeśam ca deva īśam śakram eva ca rāmeśvaram mokṣatīrtham tathā kuśa laveśvarau narmadeśam kapardīśam sāgareśam atas param dhaurādityam param tīrtham tīrtham ca apara yoni jam daśāśvamedhikam tīrtham koṭitīrtham ca sattamāḥ mārkaṇḍam brahmatīrtham ca ādivārāham uttamam āśāpūra abhidham tīrtham kauberam mārutam tathā varuṇeśam yameśam ca rāmeśam karkaṭeśvaram vaiṣṇavam cakratīrtham ca rāmakeśava saṃjñitam jayavārāhartīrtham ca tīrtham asmāhaka āhvayam aṅgāreśam ca siddheśam tapeśvaram atas param punar siddheśvaram nāma tīrtham ca varuṇeśvaram parāśara īśvaram puṇyam kusumeśam anuttamam kuṇḍaleśvara tīrtham ca tathā kalakaleśvaram nyaṅkuvārāha saṃjñam ca aṅkolam tīrtham uttamam śvetavārāhatīrtham ca bhārgalam sauram uttamam huṅkārasvāmitīrtham ca śuklatīrtham ca śobhanam saṅgamaḥ madhumatyāḥ ca tīrtham vai saṅgameśvaram narmadeśvara saṃjñam ca nadī tritaya saṅgamaḥ anekeśvara tīrtham ca śarbheśam mokṣa saṃjñitam kāverīsaṅgamaḥ puṇyaḥ tīrtham gopeśvara āhvayam mārkaṇḍa īśam ca nāga īśam udambaryāḥ ca saṅgamaḥ sāmbāditya āhvayam tīrtham udambaryāḥ ca saṅgamaḥ siddheśvaram ca mārkaṇḍam tathā siddheśvarīkṛtam bhūtīśvara āhvayam tīrtham gaṅgāvāham atas param gautameśvaratīrtham ca daśāśvamedhikam tathā bhṛgutīrtham tathā puṇyam khyātā saubhāgyasundarī vṛṣakhātam ca tatra eva kedāram dhūta pātakam tīrtham dhūteśvarīsaṅgam eraṇḍī saṃjñakam tathā tīrtham ca kanakeśvaryāḥ jvāleśvaram tatas param tathā eva udīrṇa vārāham tīrtham candraprabhāsakam dvādaśādityatīrtham ca tathā siddheśvara abhidham viśvarūpa āhvayam tīrtham nārāyaṇa kṛtam tathā mūlaśrīpatitīrtham ca caulaśrīpati saṃjñakam devatīrtham haṃsatīrtha prabhāsam tīrtham uttamam mūlasthānam ca kaṇṭha īśam aṭṭahāsam atas param bhūrbhuveśvaratīrtham ca khyātā śūleśvarī tathā sāvitrītīrtham atulam vālakhilyeśvaram tathā mad śakeśvaratīrtham ca śikhitīrtham ca śobhanam koṭitīrtham muni śreṣṭhāḥ tatra koṭīśvarī mṛḍā tatra nārāyaṇa īśam ca akrūra īśam atas param tathā eva ca mātṛtīrtham tathā punar tathā ca kurarītīrtham ḍhauṇḍheśam daśakanyakam suvarṇabindutīrtham ca ṛṇa pāpa pramocanam bhārabhūteśvaram tīrtham tathā muṇḍīśvaram viduḥ ekaśālam ḍiṇḍipāṇim tīrtham ca apsarasam param munyālayam ca mārkaṇḍam gaṇitā devatā āhvayam āmaleśvara tīrtham ca tīrtham kantheśvaram tathā āṣāḍhītīrtham iti āhuḥ śṛṅgītīrtham tathā eva ca bakeśvaratīrtham ca kapāleśam tathā eva ca mārkaṇḍam kapileśam ca eraṇḍīsaṅgamaḥ tathā eraṇḍīdevatātīrtham rāmatīrtham atas param jamadagneḥ param tīrtham revā sāgara saṅgamaḥ loṭaneśvaratīrtha tat luṅkeśa nāmakam tathā vṛṣarakhātam tatra kuṇḍam tathā eva ṛṣi sattamāḥ tathā haṃseśvaram nāma tilādam vāsaveśvaram tathā koṭīśvaram tīrtham alikātīrtham uttamam vimaleśvaratīrtham ca revā sāgara saṅgame evam tīrtha āvaliḥ puṇyā mayā proktā mahā ṛṣayaḥ tīrtha sukta āvaliḥ puṇyā grathitā taṭa rajjunā narmadā nīra nirṇiktā mārkaṇḍeya vinirmitā maṇḍanāya iha sādhūnām sarva loka hitāya ca darita dhvānta śamanī dhāryā dharma arthibhiḥ sadā ahorātra kṛtam pāpam sakṛt japtvā āśu nāśayet tri kālam japtvā māsa uttham śiva agre ca tri māsikam māsam japtvā atha varṣa uttham varṣam japtvā śata ābdikam paṭhan tīrtha āvalim puṇyām gayā śrāddha pradaḥ bhavet pūjā kāle ca devānām śraddhayā puratas paṭhan prīṇayet sarva devān ca punāti sakalam kulam evam tīrtha āvaliḥ puṇyā revā tīra dvaya āśritā mayā proktāḥ muni śreṣṭhāḥ tathā eva śṛṇuta anaghāḥ iti śrī skānde mahāpurāṇe ekāśīti sāhasryām saṃhitāyām pañcame āvantya khaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe tīrthāvalikathanam triṃśat adhika dviśatatamaḥ adhyāyaḥ śrī sūtaḥ uvāca tathā eva tīrtha stabakān vakṣye aham ṛṣi sattamāḥ yaiḥ tu tīrtha āvalī gumphaḥ pūrva uktaiḥ ekatas kṛtaḥ mṛkaṇḍa tanayaḥ pūrvam prāha pārthāya pṛcchate yathā tathā aham vakṣyāmi tīrthānām stabakān iha śiva ambu pāna jā puṇyā revā kalpalatā kila tīra dvaya udbhūta tīrtha prasūnaiḥ puṣpitā śubhā bhramaraḥ khalu mārkaṇḍaḥ muniḥ matimatām varaḥ tad puṣpa mālām hṛdaye tīrtha stabaka citritām dadhāti satatam puṇyām muniḥ bhṛgukulodvahaḥ tasyāḥ stabaka saṃsthānam vakṣye aham ṛṣi sattamāḥ saṅgamaḥ sahitāni evam revā tīra dvaye api ca triśatam śivatīrthāni trayīḥ triṃśat samanvitam mārkaṇḍeśvaratīrthāni daśa teṣu muni īśvarāḥ soma saṃsthāpitāni aṣṭau tāvantaḥ narmadā īśvarāḥ koṭitīrthāni atha aṣṭau ca sapta siddha īśvarāḥ tathā nāgeśvarāḥ ca sapta eva revā tīra dvaye api tu kedāreśvaratīrthāni pañca pañca indra jāni ca varuṇa īśāḥ ca pañca eva pañca eva dhanada īśvarāḥ deva tīrthāni pañca eva catvāraḥ vai yameśvarāḥ vaidya nāthāḥ ca catvāraḥ catvāraḥ vānara īśvarāḥ aṅgāra īśvara tīrthāni tāvanti eva muni īśvarāḥ sārasvatāni catvāri catvāraḥ dāruka īśvarāḥ gautameśvaratīrthāni trīṇi rāmeśvarāḥ trayaḥ kapāleśvaratīrthāni trīṇi haṃsa kṛtāni ca trīṇi eva mokṣatīrthāni trayaḥ vai vimaleśvarāḥ bhīmeśvarāḥ trayaḥ khyātāḥ svarṇa tīrthāni trīṇi ca dhautapāpa dvayam proktam karañjeśa dvayam tathā daśāśvamedha tīrthe dve nandītīrtha dvayam dvijāḥ vyāseśvara dvayam proktam pitṛtīrtha dvayam tathā śuklatīrtha dvayam puṇyam apsareśa dvayam tathā pippaleśvara tīrthe dve māṇḍavyeśvara saṃjñite dvīpeśvara dvayam ca eva prāha tadvat bhṛgūdvahaḥ uttareśvara tīrthe dve aśokeśa dvayī tathā luṅkeśvara dvayam khyātam ākhyānam muninā tathā stabakeṣu kṛtam tīrtham dviśatam sa caturdaśam śaivāni etāni tīrthāni vaiṣṇavāni ca sattamāḥ śṛṇudhvam procyamānāni brāhma śāktāni ca kramāt aṣṭaviṃśati tīrthāni vaiṣṇavāni abravīt muniḥ teṣu vārāhatīrthāni ṣaḍ eva muni sattamāḥ catvāri cakratīrthāni śeṣāṇi aṣṭādaśa eva hi viṣṇunā adhiṣṭhitāni eva prāha pūrvam mṛkaṇḍajaḥ triṣu ca brahmaṇaḥ pūjāḥ brahmeśāḥ caturaḥ apare aṣṭāviṃśat mayā khyātā yathāsaṅkhyam yathākramam narmadā caritam puṇyam māhātmyam muni bhāṣitam sūtaḥ uvāca yathā pārthāya saṃkṣepāt mārkaṇḍaḥ muniḥ abravīt yatra yāvat pramāṇāni tāni ākarṇayata anaghāḥ oṅkāra tīrtha paritas parvatāt amarakaṇṭāt krośa dvaye sarva dikṣu sārdha koṭī trayī matā tīrthānām saṃkhyayā gupta prakaṭānām dvijottamāḥ koṭiḥ ekā tu tīrthānām kapilā saṅgame pṛthak śatamam gāragartāyāḥ saṅgame muni sattamāḥ tīrthānām ayutam tadvat kubjāyāḥ saṅgame sthitam śatam hiraṇyagarbhāyāḥ saṅgame samavasthitam tīrthānām aṣṭaṣaṣṭiḥ ca viśokā saṅgame sthitā tathā sahasram tīrthānām saṃsthitam vāgu saṅgame śatam sarasvatī saṅge śuklatīrthe śata dvayam sahasram viṣṇutīrtheṣu mahiṣmatyām atha ayutam śūlabhede ca tīrthānām sāgram lakṣam sthitam dvijāḥ devagrāme sahasram ca tīrthānām muniḥ abravīt luṅkeśvare ca tīrthānām sāgrā saptaśatī sthitā tīrthāni aṣṭottaraśatam maṇinadyāḥ ca saṅgame vaidyanāthe ca tīrthānām śatam aṣṭa adhikam viduḥ evam tāvat pramāṇāni tīrthe kumbheśvare dvijāḥ sāgram lakṣam ca tīrthānām sthitam tatas ca api adhikāni syuḥ iti mārkaṇḍa bhāṣitam saṅgame ca karañjāyāḥ sthitam aṣṭa uttara ayutam eraṇḍīsaṅgame tadvat tīrthāni aṣṭa adhikam śatam dhūtapāpe ca tīrthānām ṣaṣṭiḥ aṣṭa adhikā sthitā skandatīrthe śatam puṇyam tīrthānām muniḥ uktavān ca tīrthānām ṣaṣṭiḥ aṣṭa adhikā sthitā sārdha koṭī ca tīrthānām sthitā vai korilāpure rāmakeśavatīrthe ca sahasram sāgram uktavān lakṣa aṣṭakam sahasre dve śuklatīrthe dvijottamāḥ tīrthāni kathayāmāsa purā pārthāya bhārgavaḥ śatam aṣṭa adhikam prāha pratyekam saṅgameṣu ca nadīnām avaśiṣṭānām kāverīsaṅgamam vinā kāveryāḥ saṅgame viprāḥ sthitā pañcaśatī tathā tīrthānām parvasu tathā viśeṣaḥ muninā uditaḥ mokṣatīrtham hi sat prāhuḥ purāṇapuruṣa āśritam bhṛgoḥ kṣetre ca tīrthānām koṭiḥ ekā samāśritā sādhikānām ṛṣi śreṣṭhāḥ vaktum śaktaḥ hi kaḥ bhavet sarva amara āśrayam proktam sarva tīrtha āśrayam tathā bhārabhūtyām ca tīrthānām sthitam aṣṭa uttaram śatam akrūreśvaratīrthe ca sa ardham tīrtha śatam sthitam daśa ayutāni tīrthānām sa adhikāni abravīt muniḥ iti śrī skānde mahāpurāṇe ekāśīti sāhasryām saṃhitāyām pañcame āvantya khaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe tīrthasaṃkhyāparigaṇanavarṇanam nāma ekatriṃśat adhika dviśatatamaḥ adhyāyaḥ sūtaḥ uvāca yathā upadiṣṭam pārthāya mārkaṇḍeyena vai purā tathā tīrtha kadambāḥ ca teṣu tīrtha viśeṣataḥ prādhānyena mayā khyātāḥ yathāsaṅkhyam yathākramam narmadā caritam puṇyam māhātmyam muni bhāṣitam sapta kalpa anugaḥ vipraḥ narmadāyām muni īśvarāḥ mṛkaṇḍa tanayaḥ dhīmān parama artha vid uttamaḥ saṃsevya sarva tīrthāni nadīḥ sarvāḥ ca vai purā bahu kalpa smarām revām ālakṣya śiva deha jām me kalā iti ca śarva uktām śaraṇam śarvajām yayau ajarām amarām devīm daitya dhvaṃsa karīm parām mahā vibhava saṃyuktām bhava ghnīm bhava jāhnavīm paritas santi sa tīrthāḥ tu sahasraśas na tulām yānti revāyāḥ tāḥ ca manye muni īśvarāḥ yat maheśa mukhāt śrutvā vāyuḥ āha ṛṣīn prati tadvat mṛkaṇḍa tanayaḥ api anubhūya akhilām nadīm sa tīrthām padaśas prāha pāṇḍu putrāya pāvanīm yadi saṃsevyate toyam revāyāḥ pāpa nāśanam mekalā jala saṃsevī muktim āpnoti śāśvatīm tāvat garjanti yajñāḥ ca vana kṣetra ādayaḥ bhṛśam garimā gāṇyate tāvat tapaḥ dāna vrata ādiṣu dhanyāḥ te deśa varyāḥ te yeṣu deśeṣu narmadā naraka anta karī śaśvat saṃśritā śarva nirmitā kṛta puṇyāḥ ca te lokāḥ śokāya na bhavanti te idam pavitram atulam revāyāḥ caritam dvijāḥ śrutaiḥ ca paṭhitaiḥ tasmāt phalam aṣṭaguṇam bhavet satra yājī phalam yat ca labhate dvādaśa ābdikam śrutvā sakṛt ca revāyāḥ caritam tat phalam labhet sarva tīrtha avagāhāt ca yat phalam sāgara ādiṣu sakṛt śrutvā ca māhātmyam revāyāḥ tat phalam labhet deśe vā maṇḍale vā api nagare grāma madhyatas gṛhe vā tiṣṭhate yasya likhitam gurūṇām ca guruḥ śāstram paramam siddhi kāraṇam dhana āḍhyaḥ jāyate vaiśyaḥ śūdraḥ vai dharma bhāj bhavet saubhāgya santatim nārī śrutvā etat samavāpnuyāt rasabhedī kṛtaghnaḥ ca mitra vañcakaḥ narmadā caritam śṛṇvan tām abdam yaḥ abhiṣevate parīvādī guroḥ pitroḥ sādhūnām nṛpateḥ tathā ye punar bhāvitātmānaḥ śastram śṛṇvanti nityaśas pūjayanti ca tat śāstram nārmadam vastra bhūṣaṇaiḥ śāstre asmin pūjite devāḥ pūjitāḥ guravaḥ tathā veda pāṭhaiḥ ca yat puṇyam agnihotraiḥ ca pālitaiḥ narmadā caritam śrutvā tat puṇyam sakalam labhet ādi madhya avasāneṣu narmadā caritam śubham samāpya śiva saṃsthānam deva kanyā samāvṛtaḥ narmadā pūjitā tena bhagavān ca maheśvaraḥ vācake pūjite tadvat devāḥ ca ṛṣayaḥ arcitāḥ bhūṣaṇam sarva śāstrāṇām yaḥ dadāti dvijanmane etat purāṇam rudra uktam mahā puṇya phala pradam dharmyam āyuṣyam atulam duḥkha duḥsvapna nāśanam yat pradattam idam puṇyam purāṇam vācyate dvijaiḥ iti śrī skānde mahāpurāṇe ekāśīti sāhasryām saṃhitāyām pañcame āvantya khaṇḍe revākhaṇḍe revākhaṇḍa samāpti revākhaṇḍa pustaka dāna ādi māhātmya varṇanam nāma dvātriṃśat adhika dviśatatamaḥ adhyāyaḥ
